# sw.models ontology index (generated)

**Stale check:** if `git -C C:/MASARepo/sw.models rev-parse --short=10 HEAD` differs from the commit above, treat this file as a hint only and confirm with grep. **Grep this file, never read it whole.**

**Callers are name matches, i.e. upper bounds.** A Lua call `x:m(` cannot be tied to a type statically, so every class with a method `m` gets the same caller list. "model" = this model's Lua (entity name, or file for top-level Lua), "sword" = sw.sword `data/app-data/resources/integration*`. A method called through a name built at runtime, or only by the kernel C++, shows no caller.

Rules checked (directai validator, see THEMES): **A** a concrete class must re-declare, non-abstract, in its own or an ancestor's XML every method of its interfaces (`class.method.unimplemented`); **B** a class `.lua` may only define methods declared in its own XML, and must define every own non-abstract one (`class.lua.method.missing-declaration` / `…unimplemented`); `<constructor>` ⇔ `__init`.
